Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a hilbert algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 891. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 2/3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to hilbert theory, we decompose the problem into 891 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in hilbert theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 342 and the invariant I(A,B) = 468 mod 2.

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the hilbert space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 342 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 468. QED.
